# Parity map

Prototype app: designs/react-reference-app
Real app: apps/platform

This file lives at `.parity/parity-map.md` and is committed with the code.
It holds one row per root pair the project has ever verified, not per
session. Ids are stable and never reused: a new pair takes the next free
`C<n>` even when an earlier row was deleted. The implementer adds and edits
rows as the work progresses; the parity verifier reads this file and never
writes it.

The prototype component is the React component name the root finder resolves
at runtime, or `root:<selector>` to address the prototype side by selector.
The real app root is a CSS selector, or the value of a `data-parity-root`
attribute the real app sets on that element; a surface built under the
workflow carries that attribute, so its row uses the value. Routes are the
real app route, then the prototype route. States are comma-separated names,
each optionally followed in parentheses by the name of a state in
`.parity/parity-states.json`, next to this map. Viewports is empty
for the full viewport set or a comma-separated `WxH` subset. Ignore is empty
or semicolon-separated
`proto:` and `real:` entries, each `hook:<data-parity value>` or
`path:<snapshot path prefix>`; only the implementer adds them, because they
change what is verified. Confidence is `obvious` for a name match, or
`confirmed` when the pairing was checked by hand or the real root carries
`data-parity-root` for that component. Source file locators belong in Notes.
Run `parity_map.py check .parity/parity-map.md` before the parity step.

Both sides carry `data-parity-root="SelectBundle"`, but the prototype puts it on its own `<main>` next to its Navbar while production's page root is a `div` inside the public shell's `<main>`, so the row pairs the content column below it (heading, subtitle, cancelled notice, selector); the call-first banner above it is row C18. The ignored paths are the hidden native `input type=radio` Radix renders inside each start-choice label because production's radio group sits in a native form; the prototype has no form, so they exist on one side only. Since the payment-link fragment change the token travels as the URL fragment on both sides (`/select-bundle#<token>`); each page reads it, strips it with `history.replaceState` and keeps it in `sessionStorage` (`coaching-sales:payment-link`) for the tab, so a reload or the cancelled-checkout return (`/select-bundle?payment=cancelled…`, no token) restores it from storage. The real side needs a live token from the fragment or that storage key; the prototype takes any Dev Toggle "Links sent by the coach" link. A `checking` state ("Checking your link…", `role="status"` `aria-busy`, no selector, no banner) precedes every resolution on both sides but is not listed: the real side leaves it as soon as the resolution POST answers, so it cannot be held deterministically; both sides cover it in their ui-integration tests. The "cancelled notice" state is reached by same-tab navigation (open a valid fragment link, then navigate to `/select-bundle?payment=cancelled&bundle=<id>&start=<choice>`) and is hand-driven because there is no navigate recipe step; both sides restore the `bundle` and `start` choices from that URL. |

Prototype `components/client-portal/onboarding/OnboardingWizard.tsx` (root `data-parity-root="OnboardingWizard"`) rendered by `pages/client-portal/ClientOnboarding.tsx`'s `OnboardingShell`; real `features/client-onboarding/ui/client/onboarding/onboarding-wizard.tsx` within `onboarding-page.tsx`'s `OnboardingShell`. `jstage=account-created` (before the `onboarding` stage) is the only journey stage whose seed gives an empty draft at `currentFormIndex: 0`; `jstage=onboarding` itself pre-seeds `goal-availability` answers and `currentFormIndex: 2` (`seedOnboarding` in `clientJourneySamples.ts`), so it was rejected as the row's route in favour of a genuinely empty form 1. Every multi-field state below is reached by the SAME shared recipe filling the domain schema's required fields with fixed values (both apps render the same `onboardingSchema`/`ONBOARDING_FORMS`, per plan U1/U4, so one recipe drives identical DOM on both sides): `onboarding-form2-consent-missing` fills form 1's required fields, continues, then continues again on form 2 without answering anything, surfacing "Tick the box to carry on." plus the unanswered safety fields' own validation; `onboarding-form3-regular-cycle` additionally clears form 2 (all safety questions "No", consent + declaration ticked) then picks "Yes, and it's regular" for `cycleRegularity` plus `hormonalContraception`/`lifeStage`/`perimenopauseOrMenopause` (the `cycleLength`/`lastPeriodStart` reveal fields have a `requires` gate on those three, so all must be answered before the reveal shows); `onboarding-form4-default` continues past form 3 with `cycleRegularity: "No, or very rarely"` (avoids the reveal fields' extra requirement) plus the form's other required fields; `onboarding-form5-default` continues past form 4 (16 required fields, all selects/radios/one text) to land on the last step. `form 1 imperial` clicks the `units-choice` "lb · in" option. GEN-207 (plan rev 3.1 U9/U10, ledger D13): the last step renders the shared progress-photo block on both sides (prototype `components/client-portal/onboarding/ProgressPhotoBlock.tsx`, real `features/client-profile/ui/shared/photos/progress-photo-block.tsx`) with the hooks `progress-photos`, `progress-photos-caption`, `progress-photos-checkbox`, `progress-photos-tiles`, `progress-photos-error`, so the tiles are verified, not ignored (the GEN-204 `Ignore` entries for `progress-photos-tiles` and `progress-photos-note` are gone). `form 5 consent ticked` (`onboarding-form5-photo-consent`, shared) is `onboarding-form5-default` plus a click on `progress-photos-checkbox`, waiting for an enabled tile input: tiles open and the send note replaces the locked note. `form 5 previews` needs a file pick, which no recipe can do on either side: run `onboarding-form5-photo-consent`, then `setInputFiles` a small PNG on `input[aria-label="Add front photo"]` (also side and back) before snapshotting; each tile shows its preview with the remove X. Picks live in component state only, never in the draft, so a reload clears them. `unsaved` (D45) is reached on `onboarding-unsaved`'s prototype side: Dev Toggle → Journey tab → "Onboarding connection" (`#dev-onboarding-connection`) → "Lost", then editing the weight field so the debounced draft PUT rejects and `save-status` reads "Not saved yet…"; the real side has no route-blocking recipe step, so the verifier hand-drives it (Playwright route interception on `PUT /api/client-onboarding/draft` before editing a field), then waits on the same `[data-parity="save-status"]` hook text. `resumed` has no reload/navigate recipe step either: on the prototype the verifier fills a field, waits for `save-status` to read "Saved", reloads the page by hand, then runs `wait-resumed` (`[data-parity="resume-note"]`); on the real side a client fixture is seeded with an existing draft (answers + `currentFormIndex`), so loading the route directly shows the resume note and `wait-resumed` alone suffices, no fill needed. `form 2 manual screening` is reached on `onboarding-manual-screening`'s prototype side: Dev Toggle → Journey tab → the new "Client age" control (`#dev-journey-age-band`) → "Under 15", then the shared form 1 fill. `needsManualScreening` reads `demoJourney.identity.dateOfBirth`, derived from `clientProfile.age`; `AppContext`'s `journeyAgeBand` dev param (`jage`, values `adult`/`under-15`/`over-69`) now overrides it (`AGE_BAND_AGES` in `ClientJourneyContext.tsx`, ages one below `PARQ_MIN_AGE` and one above `PARQ_MAX_AGE`), and the seeding effect re-runs on a live Dev Toggle change (added `journeyAgeBand` to its dependency list — previously only `journeyGender` and the other journey dev params re-seeded, so the age band would have gone stale). `onboarding-manual-screening` carries both sides: its real side is the shared form 1 fill with no dev-toggle steps (the real side has no Dev Toggle), reached from a client fixture seeded with a date of birth outside 15–69, so form 2 shows the manual-screening message as soon as it loads, same as C33's other form-2+ states. GEN-204 recheck note: the `unsaved` state's real side runs `onboarding-unsaved`'s real steps and blocks `PUT **/api/client-onboarding/draft` in the browser before it runs. The `resumed` state's real side (ledger L9) is hand-driven: fill weight 70, wait for `save-status` to read "Saved", reload, then run `wait-resumed`. |
| C34 | root:[data-parity-root="ProgramStatusCard"] | [data-parity-root="ProgramStatusCard"] | /client → /portal?session=client&jstage=submitted&jstart=immediate | submitted immediate, submitted waiting (status-waiting), in review (status-in-review), needs details (status-needs-details), approved (status-approved), waiting deadline passed (status-waiting-deadline-passed) |  |  | confirmed | GEN-204 plan U5. Prototype `components/client-portal/ProgramStatusCard.tsx` (root `data-parity-root="ProgramStatusCard"`) rendered by `pages/client-portal/ClientDashboard.tsx`; real `features/coaching-sales/ui/client/status/program-status-card.tsx` composed by `surfaces/client-portal/pages/home.tsx`. "submitted immediate" is the row's default route load (`jstart=immediate` is also `AppContext`'s default). "submitted waiting" is reached on the prototype by `status-waiting`, which drives the Dev Toggle's Journey tab "Start path" select (`#dev-journey-start-path`) to "Waiting out the 14 days" (equivalent to loading with `jstart=waiting` directly, not used here since the row's route is fixed); `realActions` is null for this state — the real side's fixture client (GEN-204 plan U7 `provisionPaidClient`) is seeded with `coaching_subscriptions.start_choice = 'waiting', paid_at = now()`, so loading `/client` directly shows the waiting line. The waiting line's date (`workStartDate`) is computed from `paid_at`/`purchasedAt` + 14 days on both sides, so the exact calendar date differs by seed/run — a content difference, not layout; do not chase digit-for-digit parity there. `start-sooner-note` and `start-now` are prototype-only (their stories land later) and ignored by hook, EXPECTED. Since the owner's review a refund is only ever the full refund of the waiting path (the Refund toggle also sets the start path to waiting), so the reason is always "Full refund: cancelled within the 14-day withdrawal period."; amounts come from the regular 3-month price €447, a third refunded in "part refunded" (€149 refunded, €298 due). Money is `en-IE` EUR, whole euros without decimals ("€447") and two decimals only when there are cents ("€148.33") (`utils/money.ts`). The "Start" reading follows start-now (it reads "Immediate start" once the waiting path is switched). GEN-208 owner review: before day 1 the period reading reads "Renews on" / "Starts when her program is delivered" (was "Once her program starts"). |

Title "Photos from {date}", description "Only you and your coach can see these photos.", the Front, Side and Back slots as `<li data-parity="photo-front\|photo-side\|photo-back">` (an empty slot reads "No {view} photo"; a stored photo is a button hooked `open-front\|side\|back`, accessible name "Open {view} photo full screen", that opens the lightbox, C66), a per-photo "Remove" button hooked `remove-front\|side\|back` (accessible name "Remove {view} photo"), and the Close footer at the end. The prototype route carries `jphotos=latest` (Dev Toggle "Seeded photos") so the latest entry already has all three photos; the real client needs that entry saved with front, side and back photos through the app (see C59). `open-photo-view` (shared) clicks `[data-parity-root="MeasurementsTable"] [data-parity="row-actions"] button` and waits for the front slot; `remove-front-photo` (shared) then clicks `remove-front`, confirms in the ConfirmDialog (C62) and waits for "No front photo", leaving a labelled empty slot with the dialog still open (the rest of the entry's photos remain). The real removal deletes the photo record and file, so restore the photo (or use a fresh entry) before each capture of "client view". "remove failed": `remove-failed` is prototype-only (drop `realActions`: set it to `remove-front-photo` with its last `waitFor` replaced by `[data-sonner-toast]` for this state); it sets the Dev Toggle Journey tab "Photo removal" `#dev-photo-removal` to "Fails" (URL `jremove=fails`), opens the view, clicks `remove-front` and confirms; the confirm closes, the front photo stays with its Remove button and the toast "The photo could not be removed. Try again." shows (snapshot the dialog; the toast is outside this root). On the real side Playwright intercepts `DELETE **/api/client-profile/photos/*` with a 500 (`page.route`) before the same clicks, so the photo is not removed and needs no restore. Photo pixels are content (prototype placeholder SVG, real re-encoded JPEG). The coach's view is C65. Real side seed (every GEN-207 client row unless stated): a signed-in client past onboarding with her profile row, as C34: `insertSubmittedClientRecords` in `apps/platform/e2e/support/submitted-clients.ts` (submission with consent `progress_photos_consented_at` null, one measurement entry recorded at submission from `FIRST_MEASUREMENTS`: 66.1 kg, waist 74, hips 98, thigh 57, arm 28 cm, then `recordClientProfile`), which matches the prototype's MVP seed (`seedMeasurements` keeps one entry; the last entry of its three-entry post-MVP history has the same readings). |

Prototype `components/client-portal/SubscriptionSection.tsx` on `SettingsSection` (`parityRoot="SubscriptionSection"` on the `<section>`, h2 "Subscription" with the `CreditCard` glyph, no description line); real `features/coaching-sales/ui/client/settings/subscription-section.tsx` on `packages/ui`'s settings frame. Rows (each a `SettingsRow`, hooked on the row container): `subscription-plan` ("{N months} of coaching" over the dates: "Paid d MMMM · starts when your program is delivered." before day 1, "Active until d MMMM · renews then unless you cancel first." after day 1, "Paid d MMMM · cancelled d MMMM · access until d MMMM." once cancelled; there is no renewal row), `subscription-cancellation` (title "Cancellation", the rule's facts, and the `destructive-outline` sm action hooked `cancel-action` reading "Cancel" on both rules), `subscription-payment-method` (title "Payment method", the card on file as the row's description, and the outline sm `manage-payment-method` button reading "Change", no icon). Both row actions share `w-full sm:w-28`, so they line up as one column from 640px and fill the row below it; each names its row through `aria-describedby` (Cancel: the row title and facts ids; Change: the row title, the card reading's description id and any problem line ids), so a screen reader hears "Cancel, Cancellation, …". The dialogs keep the rule's label as title and confirm. The rule is binary: the waiting path before purchase + 14 days cancels with a full refund (dialog "Cancel and get a full refund"); everything else cancels without one (dialog "Cancel subscription"). The `SettingsRow` primitive's `problem` slot (a `mt-1` div under the description) holds, in a `grid gap-1 pt-1` (8px under the card reading), the payment problem line in the shared `InlineProblem` style with `role="status"` hooked `payment-problem` ("Your last payment didn't go through. Update your card to keep your coaching going.") and a failed hand-off `InlineProblem` with `role="alert"` hooked `payment-method-problem`. The section is hidden once the subscription has ended; a cancelled one drops the Cancellation and Payment method rows. States (prototype-only Dev Toggle drives on the Journey tab, drop `realActions`; real side seeds one client per state): "waiting-refundable" is the route load (waiting path, paid 5 days ago: "Until d MMMM you can cancel for a full refund. Your access ends right away."); "no-refund" sets "Days since payment" `#dev-journey-days-since-payment` to "14 days (withdrawal right gone)" (URL `jpaid=14`; "You won't be charged again, there is no refund for the coaching already paid, and your access stays until d MMMM.", the date being purchase + 3 months; the immediate path reads the same at any time); "cancelled" sets "Subscription state" to "Cancelled"; "payment problem" ticks "Payment problem" (`jpayproblem=1`). Dates are content. GEN-208 Draft 3 (D82–D84, ledger design change D20): the Payment method row's description `p` (`mt-0.5 text-xs text-text-secondary`, id `subscription-payment-card`) holds the card on file as phrasing content from prototype `components/client-portal/CardOnFileReading.tsx`: `payment-card` is a `mt-2 flex items-center gap-3` span with the shared `CardBrandMark` (prototype `components/CardBrandMark.tsx`, hooked `payment-card-mark`: an `aria-hidden` 48×32 `flex overflow-hidden rounded-tile border border-border-subtle bg-surface-base text-text-secondary` tile holding a `size-full` inline SVG on a `0 0 46 30` viewBox, the tile's inner box: the Visa wordmark in `#1A1F71`, the Mastercard circles `#EB001B` / `#F79E1B` with the `#FF5F00` overlap, or a neutral `currentColor` card outline for any other brand; brand colours are literal inside the SVG, `data-mark` names the drawn mark) beside a `grid` of two lines; the first line (`flex items-baseline gap-2`, the data value class `text-sm font-medium text-text-primary`) has `payment-card-brand` (`font-semibold`, the brand as a word: "Visa", "Mastercard", "American Express", …) and `payment-card-number` (`tabular-nums`, an `aria-hidden` "•••• 4242" with U+2022 bullets and a `sr-only` "ending in 4242"); the second line is `payment-card-expiry` (`tabular-nums`, inherits the description's xs secondary ink: "Expires 12/34"). The Change button's accessible description reads "Payment method Visa ending in 4242 Expires 12/34" (plus any problem line). "card on file" is the route load (Dev Toggle Journey tab "Card on file" `#dev-journey-card-on-file` defaults to "Visa •••• 4242, 12/34", URL `jcard`; `mastercard` reads "Mastercard •••• 4444" / "Expires 03/31"); "card on file mastercard" (`card-mastercard`, prototype-only, drop `realActions`) picks "Mastercard •••• 4444, 03/31" (`jcard=mastercard`) and draws the Mastercard circles; "no card" (`no-card`, prototype-only, drop `realActions`) picks "No card" (`jcard=none`) and the description reads "No payment method configured" as a plain `payment-card` span with no mark. Real side: a client whose mirrored card is Visa 4242 12/34 for "card on file", one whose `payment_cards` row holds brand mastercard, 4444, 03/2031 for "card on file mastercard", and one with no mirrored card for "no card". The payment problem line sits under the card. |

The root takes the first row holding a struck-through time; on the Requests tab the rows waiting for her come first on both sides. Expected drift: ck-10 is a recurring check-in (story 5), so the prototype's when label has no kind ("New time from your coach") where production reads "Ad-hoc · New time from your coach". Real seed (GEN-219, add to the C98 and C100 seed in a throwaway database `eli_coach_platform_gen219`, migrated; never the shared `eli_coach_platform`): the signed-in client is named Jane Doe (the prototype demo client `c1`), and four of her check-ins are inserted directly into `app.check_ins` (kind `ad_hoc`, `client_time_zone` and `coach_time_zone` `Europe/Bucharest`, each with its `check_in` row in `app.coach_time_reservations`) on the prototype seed's days of the capture week, wall times in Europe/Bucharest: (ck-10) pending, `initiated_by` and `proposed_by` `coach`, starts Thursday two weeks out 14:00, `earlier_starts_at` Wednesday two weeks out 10:00, `moves` 1, `note` null, `new_time_note` "I am travelling that Wednesday. Could we do Thursday at 2 PM instead?"; (ck-15) pending, `initiated_by` `coach`, `proposed_by` `client`, starts Friday two weeks out 16:00, `earlier_starts_at` the same Friday 10:00, `moves` 1, `note` "Time to look back at your first month", `new_time_note` "I have a work call that morning. Could we meet on Friday afternoon?"; (ck-11) approved, `initiated_by` `client`, `proposed_by` `coach`, starts Friday this week 12:00, `earlier_starts_at` Thursday this week 11:00, `moves` 1, `note` "Want to go over my food log", `new_time_note` null; (ck-16) approved, `initiated_by` and `proposed_by` `client`, starts Thursday next week 16:00, `earlier_starts_at` Tuesday next week 15:00, `moves` 2, `note` "Can we plan my holiday training?", `new_time_note` "Thursday afternoon suits me better.". Moved check-ins cannot be arranged in the past through the API, and a direct insert keeps the four states stable between captures; reseed before each state that changes them (proposal, cancellation, acceptance). |

Real: the create must not persist, or the shared seed gains a third recipe for good (production has no recipe delete before story 5): before the created toast's steps the verifier routes the builder's save, `page.route(/\/api\/coach\/recipes\.data$/, …)` for `POST` only, and fulfils it without reaching the server with status 200, `content-type: text/x-script`, header `x-remix-response: yes` and the body `[{"_1":2},"data",{"_3":4},"redirectTo","/coach/nutrition?tab=recipes"]` plus a newline (the single-fetch answer the server gives a successful save); the builder then shows the toast and returns to the Recipes tab as after a real create. The prototype keeps its recipes in memory per page load, so no route is needed there. Seed as C228. |
